      "solution": "**Identify.** DNA fragments are:\n\n**Concept.** DNA is negatively charged because of phosphate groups in the backbone — the basis of agarose gel electrophoresis toward the anode.\n\n**Work.** Charge sign does not flip with fragment size; size affects migration speed, not polarity.\n\n**Option scan.**\n✗ (1) Positively charged\n✓ (2) Negatively charged\n✗ (3) Neutral\n✗ (4) Either positively or negatively charged depending on their size\n\n**Hence proved.** The verified option wording is **(2) Negatively charged**.\n\n**Eliminate.**\n✗ (1) Positively charged — DNA is not positively charged.\n✗ (3) Neutral — Phosphate groups make DNA anionic, not neutral.\n✗ (4) Either positively or negatively charged depending on their size — Size changes mobility, not the sign of charge.",
